Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The lists variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 542 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 252 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the lists decomposition. By minimality, the element a^542 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 252-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
